# Agents · S · part 27

1,031 entries, specialist-database to spotter. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **specialist-database**: Owns schema design, migrations, query optimisation, and data integrity rules. Works with PostgreSQL, MySQL, SQLite, and common ORMs (Prisma, SQLAlchemy, Drizzle). Reads database learnings before every task and appends new patterns after every non-trivial implementation. ([tylerbjones1504-hash/Agents](https://github.com/tylerbjones1504-hash/Agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-database.md) · 3 copies)
- **specialist-developer-experience**: Developer experience specialist. Deep expertise in SDKs, extensibility, and developer adoption. Route here for "can we build on top of this" questions, SDK/API-ergonomics questions, and developer-onboarding conversations. ([migos1990/presales-harness-starter](https://github.com/migos1990/presales-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/specialist-developer-experience.md) · ★2)
- **specialist-esbuild**: PER-TECH SPECIALIST. esbuild / bundling expert for VS Code extensions. Use when a task hinges on the build pipeline — esbuild config, the dist/extension.js output, cjs format, marking vscode external, watch mode, sourcemaps, tree-shaking, or bundle-size issues. Carries the tech-esbuild.md rule and verifies current esbuild API via Context7 / official docs. ([ocx-sh/vscode-ocx](https://github.com/ocx-sh/vscode-ocx/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-esbuild.md))
- **specialist-frontend**: Implements a bounded frontend task when Build explicitly delegates UI, routes, components, styling, accessibility, or frontend API integration. ([ekasc/dotfiles](https://github.com/ekasc/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/specialist-frontend.md) · ★2)
- **specialist-frontend**: Owns all UI work: React/Vue/Svelte components, CSS/Tailwind, state management, accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA), and client-side performance. Reads frontend learnings before every task and appends new patterns after every non-trivial implementation. ([tylerbjones1504-hash/Agents](https://github.com/tylerbjones1504-hash/Agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-frontend.md) · 3 copies)

- **specialist-integrations**: Integrations specialist. Deep expertise in APIs, connectors, ecosystem coverage, and data flow between systems. Route here for "does this connect to X" questions, connector-catalog gaps, and integration-architecture questions. ([migos1990/presales-harness-starter](https://github.com/migos1990/presales-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/specialist-integrations.md) · ★2)
- **specialist-judge**: Universal arbiter for the `specialist` skill. Serves every specialist in every discipline. Invoked by the skill in two modes: TRIAGE (decide the minimal set of specialists a request needs — never "all" by default) and ARBITRATION (reconcile the specialists' reports, confront divergences, and rule on each open doubt: bounce back to a specialist, escalate to the user, or record as a gap). Read-only: decides and reports, never writes and never dispatches (the skill executes its rulings). ([felipe-venturini/mind-plus-plus](https://github.com/felipe-venturini/mind-plus-plus/blob/HEAD/agents/core__specialist_judge.md))
- **specialist-k8s**: ../../.claude/agents/specialist-k8s.md ([rb1whitney/agentic-project-factory](https://github.com/rb1whitney/agentic-project-factory/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/specialist-k8s.md))
- **specialist-literature**: Literature search & synthesis specialist (mirrors Claude Science's literature capability). Searches PubMed / Europe PMC / arXiv, dedupes, synthesizes by theme, and extracts citable conclusions each carrying a DOI/PMID. Use when a task needs a literature review or supporting evidence for a claim. ([JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win](https://github.com/JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-literature.md) · ★37)

- **specialist-performance**: Identify performance issues introduced by the PR diff that have measurable, evidence-backed impact. Do not speculate. Only flag a performance concern when the diff contains concrete evidence: a loop over an unbounded collection calling a network or disk operation, a query inside a loop (N+1), a blocking call on an async path, or removal of a critical cache. For each finding, state the evidence fro ([thisisqubika/ai-mast-challenge-promptazo](https://github.com/thisisqubika/ai-mast-challenge-promptazo/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/pr-reviewer/agents/specialist-performance.md) · 2 copies)
- **specialist-phylogenetics**: Phylogenetics & evolution specialist (mirrors Claude Science's phylogenetics capability). Builds multiple-sequence alignments and trees, runs selection and ancestral-reconstruction analyses, and presents results alongside the sequences that produced them. Use for sequence alignment, phylogenetic trees, selection analysis. ([JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win](https://github.com/JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-phylogenetics.md) · ★37)

Use for protein structure, PDB, structure comparison, ESM/protein language models, structure prediction. - **specialist-scale**: Scale specialist. Deep expertise in performance, reliability, and scale characteristics, including SLA and uptime commitments. ([tylerbjones1504-hash/Flint](https://github.com/tylerbjones1504-hash/Flint/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/specialist-scoring.md))
- **specialist-security**: Security specialist. Deep expertise in security posture, data protection, and compliance framework alignment (SOC 2, ISO 27001, GDPR). ([migos1990/presales-harness-starter](https://github.com/migos1990/presales-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/specialist-security.md) · ★2)

- **specialist-tests**: Identify gaps in test coverage introduced or revealed by the PR diff. Flag new public API surface, new business logic branches, and new error paths that have no corresponding test. Flag tests that assert the wrong thing, are brittle, or would pass even if the implementation were broken. Do not flag pre-existing test gaps that the PR did not touch. ([thisisqubika/ai-mast-challenge-promptazo](https://github.com/thisisqubika/ai-mast-challenge-promptazo/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/pr-reviewer/agents/specialist-tests.md) · 2 copies)

Use when writing/reviewing/debugging Dockerfiles, Compose files, image builds (BuildKit/buildx), container networking and volumes, or runtime/security config. - **specialist:guix**: Expert in GNU Guix, the functional package manager and the Guix System distribution. Use when writing, reviewing, or debugging package definitions, services, home configurations, channels, manifests, G-expressions, build phases, importers/updaters, or operating-system declarations; when working through `guix build`/`shell`/`pack`/`pull`/`system`/`home` failures; or when diagnosing substitutes, the store, the daemon, or grafts. Scheme/Guile-specific; pairs with general engineering agents for surrounding language work in the package being packaged. Use when configuring, reviewing, or debugging HAProxy: the global/defaults/frontend/backend/listen structure, http vs tcp mode, ACLs and maps, stick-tables and peers, load-balancing algorithms, server directives and health checks, SSL/TLS termination with crt-store/crt-list and SNI, HTTP/2 and HTTP/3 (QUIC), http-request/http-response rules and converters, rate limiting… ([franzos/claude-plugins](https://github.com/franzos/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/infra/agents/specialist-haproxy.md) · ★1)

- **speckit.product-forge.api-docs**: Generates OpenAPI 3.1 spec and Postman collection from plan.md API contracts. Auto-detects existing API framework (NestJS, Express, FastAPI, etc.), validates consistency between spec and implementation, and outputs ready-to-publish docs. Use after implement: "generate API docs", "/speckit.product-forge.api-docs" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.api-docs.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.backfill**: Brown-field entry point: reverse-engineer a Product Forge feature folder from existing code. Reads a target module/directory, synthesises retro product-spec, plan, and simplified tasks, then writes `.forge-status.yml` with all phases marked `completed` and `backfilled: true`. Produces a gaps-report.md listing what a modern Product Forge run would have required but is currently missing (tests, tracking, ADRs). Use: "backfill module", "create feature from existing code", "/speckit.product-forge.backfill" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.backfill.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.bridge**: Phase 4: Converts approved product-spec artifacts into a SpecKit-compatible spec.md, enriched with full research and product context. Then launches SpecKit in classic mode (plan → tasks → implement). Use with: "bridge to speckit", "create spec", "/speckit.product-forge.bridge" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.bridge.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.change-request**: Cross-cutting scope change management. When requirements change mid-lifecycle, captures the change formally, analyzes impact across all artifacts, estimates effort delta, and propagates approved changes. Runs sync-verify after application. Use: "change request", "scope change", "add requirement", "/speckit.product-forge.change-request" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.change-request.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.code-review**: Phase 6B: Structured multi-agent code review after implementation, before verification. Checks code quality (SOLID, DRY), security (OWASP surface scan), pattern consistency (vs codebase-analysis.md), and test coverage (vs spec.md requirements). Enriched with Product Forge context — not a generic code review. Use: "code review", "review code", "/speckit.product-forge.code-review" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.code-review.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.design-system-harvest**: Phase 2 helper: discover the project's EXISTING design system (component library, design tokens, Storybook, Tailwind/CSS config) and emit a read-only manifest that grounds mockups, component decomposition, and verification in real code. The design system stays in code as the single source of truth; this command harvests a manifest, it never duplicates or rewrites the design system. Use with: "harvest design system", "/speckit.product-forge.design-system-harvest" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.design-system-harvest.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.experiment-design**: Optional Phase 9B: design a rigorous A/B experiment plan before shipping a feature behind a flag. Produces hypothesis, primary/secondary/guardrail metrics, minimum detectable effect, sample size calculation, exposure rules, and pre-registered decision thresholds. Wraps the installed `feature-flag-ab-testing` skill (or equivalent). Runs at Phase 9B — after Phase 9.5 (Monitoring Setup) and before deploy, when a flag-gated feature will be A/B tested. Use: "design experiment", "A/B plan", "/speckit.product-forge.experiment-design" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.experiment-design.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.feature-flag-cleanup**: Cross-cutting stale-flag audit. Scans every `flags/registry.yml` produced by release-readiness across all features, cross-checks against the actual codebase, and produces a cleanup plan for flags whose `cleanup_after` date has passed. Wraps the installed `feature-flag-manager` skill. Safe to run on any schedule; read-only by default. Use: "clean up flags", "stale flags", "/speckit.product-forge.feature-flag-cleanup" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.feature-flag-cleanup.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.forge**: Full lifecycle orchestrator for Product Forge v1.6.0. Drives a feature from idea to shipped, measured code through the phase map selected by `feature_mode` (express / lite / standard) with human-in-the-loop gates, cross-artifact sync-verify between transitions, and a complete gate audit trail. Standard phases: research → product-spec → revalidation → bridge → plan → tasks → pre-impl-review → implement → code-review → verify → test-plan → test-run → release-readiness. Lite phases: problem-discovery (opt) → product-spec → plan → implement → verify. Use with: "forge feature", "run full cycle", "… ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.forge.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.i18n-harvest**: Optional post-bridge step: extract every user-facing string from wireframes, product-spec, and (if available) UI prototypes, generate locale key stubs namespaced by the feature slug, and create TODO entries in every configured locale file. Wraps the project's `i18n-workflow` skill where available. Runs at Phase 4.5 — between Phase 4 (Bridge) and Phase 5 (Plan). Use: "harvest i18n keys", "i18n stubs", "/speckit.product-forge.i18n-harvest" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.i18n-harvest.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.implement**: Phase 6: Execute implementation from tasks.md with progressive verification. Delegates to SpecKit implement, monitors task completion, runs mini-verify every N tasks, surfaces product-spec context to implementation agents. Standalone — run after pre-impl-review (or after any custom step inserted before coding). Use: "implement", "start coding", "/speckit.product-forge.implement" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.implement.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.migration-plan**: Optional pre-implement phase: when plan.md introduces data-model changes, generate a zero-downtime migration plan with forward + rollback scripts, validation queries, backfill job outline, and risk matrix. Wraps the installed `db-migration-manager` and `mongodb-ops` skills. Runs at Phase 5.5 — after Phase 5B (Tasks) and before Phase 5C (Pre-Impl Review), when schema changes are detected in plan.md. Use: "migration plan", "schema migration", "/speckit.product-forge.migration-plan" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.migration-plan.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.monitoring-setup**: Phase 9.5 (optional): produce real monitoring artifacts for a feature — a dashboard, alert rules, and SLI/SLO definitions derived from plan NFRs and tracking-plan events. Targets the configured backend (`telemetry.dashboards`: PostHog / Sentry; NewRelic optional via the `newrelic-dashboard-builder` skill). When the matching MCP is connected, creates the dashboard/alerts directly; otherwise writes provider-neutral artifact stubs. Runs after release-readiness, before deploy. Use: "monitoring setup", "build dashboard", "/speckit.product-forge.monitoring-setup" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.monitoring-setup.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.plan**: Phase 5: Generate technical plan from spec.md with cross-validation against product-spec. Ensures plan covers all Must Have user stories and aligns with codebase analysis. Standalone — run independently, before tasks or implement. Use: "create plan", "technical plan", "/speckit.product-forge.plan" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.plan.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.portfolio**: Cross-cutting portfolio view over every feature managed by Product Forge. Reads every feature's `.forge-status.yml` (via the Path-Resolution Contract's `enumerate()`, so flat and domain-nested layouts both work) plus optional `tasks.md`, and produces a consolidated report covering feature status, file/module conflicts between in-flight features, a dependency graph, and a suggested merge order. Use: "show portfolio", "feature portfolio", "what's blocked", "/speckit.product-forge.portfolio" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.portfolio.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.pre-impl-review**: Phase 5C: Combined design review, architecture review, and risk assessment gate before writing any code. Loads wireframes, UX patterns, plan architecture, and codebase analysis to produce a structured review with risk register. Optional for features with &lt;=5 tasks and no UI. Use: "pre-impl review", "review before coding", "/speckit.product-forge.pre-impl-review" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.pre-impl-review.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.problem-discovery**: Phase 0: Validates the problem before any research begins. Runs JTBD analysis, generates an interview script, builds a Problem Statement Canvas, and outputs a scored confidence report. Prevents investing in features that solve the wrong problem. Use before research: "discover the problem", "/speckit.product-forge.problem-discovery" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.problem-discovery.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.product-spec**: Phase 2: Interactive product specification creation. Asks detailed questions about desired level of detail and document decomposition. Creates product-spec.md, structured journeys (journeys/journeys.yml as the E2E source of truth), wireframes, metrics.md, and design-system-grounded clickable mockups with a component map. All documents are cross-linked via product-spec/README.md index. Use with: "create product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.product-spec" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.product-spec.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.release-readiness**: Phase 9: Pre-ship readiness checklist. Covers feature flags, rollout strategy, rollback plan, documentation, monitoring, analytics, and deployment dependencies. Consolidates api-docs, tracking-plan, and security-check status into one gate. Optional for internal/backend-only features. Use: "release readiness", "ready to ship?", "/speckit.product-forge.release-readiness" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.release-readiness.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.research**: Phase 1: Adaptive multi-dimensional feature research. Assesses input richness and auto-adjusts interview depth — minimal input triggers full 7-question interview, rich input skips to confirmation. Mandatory: competitors, UX/UI, codebase. Optional: tech stack, metrics/ROI. Saves to features/&lt;name&gt;/research/. Use with: "research feature", "/speckit.product-forge.research" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.research.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.retrospective**: Post-launch retrospective comparing predicted metrics from research/metrics-roi.md against real data pulled from connected MCPs (PostHog / Amplitude product analytics, Sentry error tracking, NewRelic APM) or manual input. Closes the loop on the full product lifecycle. Run 2+ weeks after shipping. Use: "retrospective", "/speckit.product-forge.retrospective {feature-slug}" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.retrospective.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.revalidate**: Phase 3: Iterative revalidation and correction cycle. Shows product spec summary, collects user feedback in chat, applies corrections via a dedicated agent, and loops until explicit user approval. All revision history recorded in review.md. Use with: "revalidate spec", "review product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.revalidate" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.revalidate.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.security-check**: Feature-specific OWASP security audit. Reads plan.md to detect auth, payments, user input, and file handling — then checks only the relevant attack vectors. Generates a prioritized fix list with code-level evidence. Run after implement, before ship. Use: "security check", "/speckit.product-forge.security-check" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.security-check.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.spec-merge**: Cross-cutting (living spec): merge a completed feature's delta specs (ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED requirements) into the canonical specs/ source of truth, then archive the change folder with audit history. Makes the spec spec-anchored — a living source of truth across the feature's lifetime, not a per-change artifact. Use with: "merge spec", "archive change", "/speckit.product-forge.spec-merge" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.spec-merge.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.status**: Show the current Product Forge lifecycle status for a feature. Reads .forge-status.yml, displays all phases with completion state, lists available artifacts, and recommends the next action. Works for any feature in the features/ directory. Use with: "forge status", "show status", "/speckit.product-forge.status" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.status.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.sync-verify**: Cross-cutting artifact consistency checker. Verifies alignment across ALL lifecycle layers — research ↔ product-spec ↔ spec.md ↔ plan.md ↔ tasks.md ↔ code. Detects forward drift (earlier artifacts not reflected in later) and backward drift (later decisions that should update earlier artifacts). Runnable between ANY phases. Human-in-the-loop for every CRITICAL/WARNING resolution. Use: "sync-verify", "check consistency", "/speckit.product-forge.sync-verify" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.sync-verify.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.tasks**: Phase 5B: Generate task breakdown from plan.md with cross-validation against product-spec. Ensures every Must Have story and functional requirement has at least one task. Standalone — run after plan, before implement (or after any custom step). Use: "generate tasks", "task breakdown", "/speckit.product-forge.tasks" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.tasks.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.test-plan**: Phase 8A: Creates a comprehensive test plan from feature artifacts. Asks about test types (smoke, E2E Playwright, API/integration, regression), environment setup, credentials, auto-detects project test framework, and generates test cases mapped to user stories. Saves to features/&lt;name&gt;/testing/. Use with: "create test plan", "/speckit.product-forge.test-plan" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.test-plan.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.test-run**: Phase 8B: Executes test cases via playwright-cli (interactive browser agent), tracks bugs in bugs/&lt;BUG-NNN&gt;.md, auto-fixes P0/P1 bugs and retests, performs gap analysis when bugs require spec changes. Loop continues until all P0/P1 bugs closed and exit criteria met. Use with: "run tests", "execute tests", "/speckit.product-forge.test-run" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.test-run.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.tracking-plan**: Generates an analytics tracking plan from user journeys and product-spec. Creates event taxonomy, property schemas, funnel definitions, and ready-to-paste code snippets for your analytics SDK. Links each event to a user story and success metric. Use after product-spec: "tracking plan", "/speckit.product-forge.tracking-plan" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.tracking-plan.agent.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.verify-full**: Phase 7: Full traceability verification across the entire Product Forge chain. Consumes the live traceability.yml matrix and checks: code ↔ tasks ↔ plan ↔ spec.md ↔ product-spec ↔ research, plus journey↔E2E coverage, UI↔design-system, FE↔BE contract drift, and doc↔code reconciliation. Produces a structured verify-report.md with CRITICAL/WARNING/PASSED findings. Use with: "verify full", "check traceability", "/speckit.product-forge.verify-full" ([radicle-co/KitchenSink](https://github.com/radicle-co/KitchenSink/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.product-forge.verify-full.agent.md))
- **speckit.pullrequest.agent**: Manage a pull request from creation through feedback, mergeability, and CI until ready to merge. ([jahales/etude-story](https://github.com/jahales/etude-story/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.pullrequest.agent.md))

- **spk-cr-verifier**: Stage 6 of the spk-code-review pipeline. Exists to reduce false positives. ([sparkgeo/sparkgeo-ai](https://github.com/sparkgeo/sparkgeo-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/spk-code-review/agents/spk-cr-verifier.md))
